## §0A EXECUTIVE CONSENSUS — WHY THESE ADVANCE

**The book in three lines**

- What they share: seven of the eleven (FLYW, AJG, KDP, MA, PFE, ABBV, CNQ) sit at their 52-week high, all eleven beat the market, and every entry is an hourly close above Friday's high, so nothing is a buy on the open print.
- Agreement was strongest on FLYW, AJG and KDP (unanimous from the first pass) and EL (no reservations at all); it was thinnest on MA and TTEK, both carried over a real objection that the price is a chase.
- The group thesis dies if the market stops paying breakouts: the Russell 2000 under 2,804.16, SPY under its 750 put wall, or Valen's net-new-highs switch (8-day 5.5 over 20-day -2.6) turning back.

**The process read (the QA seat)**

- Every analyst read the fields and none recited, but 151 floor messages moved no stance, and 105 ratification answers carried 0 objections against 30 reservations.
- Several reservations were disagreements filed softly (chase calls on MA, TTEK and SN; dropped pullback entries on ABBV, ECL, SN, CNQ; the missing tighten line on V), and each trigger line lists every analyst's own words, so each finds itself in it: that is breadth, not convergence.
- The analysts' own cases name a RISK OFF / downtrend tape that the slate never printed; the hold round reopened MA, TTEK and V only, and all three calls stood.

### FLYW — ADVANCE · conviction 4
Advance: a fresh break to its 52-week high on 57% more volume than normal (1.57x), RS rank 89.17, structure 83.2, with the 20-, 50-, 100- and 200-day averages in order; unanimous. The losing argument is that the pace of the advance is slowing (mp 82, decelerating) and no case printed the market condition it depends on in a RISK OFF tape. It has to hold the old pivot, 19.54, on a daily close.

### AJG — ADVANCE · conviction 4
Advance: the highest-structure break in the room (89.5) to a new 52-week high on 2.16x volume, RS rank 86.25, in Insurance, one of the true-leader themes; unanimous. The losing argument: the base was never confirmed tight, so the case is leadership and volume, not a proven base. A daily close under 271.27 ends it.

### KDP — ADVANCE · conviction 4
Advance: a Staples breakout to a new 52-week high on 2.52x volume with force accelerating (mp 97), in a sector up 11.04% over 20 days; unanimous. The losing argument: Friday's bar did not close as a clean strength bar, so only an hourly close above 36.13 on volume qualifies. Dead on a daily close under 35.40.

### MA — ADVANCE · conviction 3
Advance, carried over a real objection: the pullback already resolved (Elder 7, 6, 5 then 9, 10), force is accelerating (mp 97) and structure scores 84.2 at the 52-week high, so the winning side buys an hourly close above 644.24. The losing argument, held firmly: at 638.01 it is 6.12% past the pivot and 8.74% over its 20-day after a red bar, a chase, and it would clear on a pull-in toward the 20-day at 606.28, then an hourly close above that pause's high on 1.4x volume. It also doubles held V's Financials exposure, and the widest exit is a daily close under the 50-day, 586.73.

### TTEK — ADVANCE · conviction 3
Advance, carried over a real objection: AQE's number-2 name, flow 100, mp 93 accelerating, 1.73x volume, inside 5% of its pivot, a higher low that turned up with thrust. The losing argument, held firmly: 9.29% under its 52-week high and 4.12 ATRs stretched with the 20-day (35.11) still under the 50-day (35.38), a mid-range break, and it would clear on a tight pause holding above 35.38 or a test of 37.27, then an hourly close above that bar's high. It reports on 11 Nov, inside the horizon.

### PFE — ADVANCE · conviction 4
Advance: the least-stretched fresh break in the room (6.91% over its 20-day), a new 52-week high with force accelerating, Healthcare leading and ABBV breaking alongside; near-unanimous. The losing argument: the Elder pattern was interrupted at the high and two oscillators show a bearish divergence, so a new high on fading volume ends it. Dead under 30.44, and ABBV must hold its own break.

Advance, in the room on a reserved reclaim seat; no voice nominated it: it dipped under its 9-day average (107.15) and closed back above it, on the heaviest volume in the room (3.07x), RS rank 88.44. The losing argument: Elder has read a flat 7 for five sessions with no transition, and a trailing P/E of 214.2 gives no earnings floor. It is dead on a daily close under Friday's low, 105.44.

### ABBV — ADVANCE · conviction 4
Advance: a Healthcare leader at a new 52-week high with force building (mp 94, accelerating), RS rank 90.15, that opened every machine door, paired with PFE; near-unanimous. The losing argument: 1.32x volume is under the 1.40 breakout bar, so the break was not bought in size, and an unproven coil means no follow-through by the third session is an exit. Advance: above its old structure with the averages in order, RS rank 73.11 and force accelerating (mp 90) in a leading Materials sector; near-unanimous. The losing argument: 1.22x volume is under the 1.40 breakout bar, coil and institutional money warn, and no second Materials name is breaking with it. The too-late line (305.76) sits just above the buy price (305.71), so a gap through it is a miss, not a chase.

### SN — ADVANCE · conviction 4

The losing argument: 13.72% over its 20-day with no setup at the close on 1.2x volume, so part of the room reads the break above 211.48 as a chase and prefers the first pullback toward 193.00. ### CNQ — ADVANCE · conviction 4
Advance, in the room on a reserved reclaim seat; no voice nominated it: it reclaimed its rising trendline at 55.37 and is the top Energy name by AQE rank (27), at a new 52-week high; near-unanimous. The losing argument: 1.35x volume sits just under the 1.40 bar, momentum is flat and no second Energy name is making a new high, in a sector with a macro headwind. It has DRIFTED +2.37% since the 29 Oct advance (1.0x its own risk), so that entry is gone; dead under 55.35.

6. Watch-only: HOG, TAK, GEN (holds) need a pull-in first; HAYW and KSS surfaced through the doors and no analyst picked them.
